     Map(\"original_type\" -> value.getClass.getSimpleName) ++ formatOptions\n    )\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n  * Tokenizer for date/time types.\n  *\n  * @param formatStr optional custom format string\n  */\nclass TemporalTokenizer(formatStr: Option[String] = None) extends BaseTokenizer[java.time.temporal.Temporal] {\n\n  /**\n    * Tokenize a date or time value.\n    *\n    * @param value the date/time to tokenize\n    * @return Token with TEMPORAL type\n    */\n  override def tokenize(value: java.time.temporal.Temporal): Token = {\n    val str = value match {\n      case ldt: LocalDateTime => formatStr.getOrElse(\"yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ss\")\n      case ld: LocalDate => formatStr.getOrElse(\"yyyy-MM-dd\")\n      case _ => formatStr.getOrElse(\"yyyy-MM-dd\")\n    }\n\n    val formatter = java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern(str)\n    Token(formatter.format(value), TokenType.TEMPORAL)\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Universal Tokenizer with Pattern Matching\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n  * Universal tokenizer that handles multiple types through pattern matching.\n  * Uses type classes internally for extensibility.\n  */\nclass UniversalTokenizer {\n  private val stringTokenizer = new StringTokenizer()\n  private val numericTokenizer = new NumericTokenizer()\n  private val temporalTokenizer = new TemporalTokenizer()\n\n  /**\n    * Tokenize any value by dispatching to appropriate tokenizer.\n    *\n    * @param value the value to tokenize\n    * @return Token with appropriate type\n    */\n  def tokenize(value: Any): Token = value match {\n    case null => Token(\"NULL\", TokenType.NULL)\n    case None => Token(\"NULL\", TokenType.NULL)\n    case Some(inner) => tokenize(inner)\n\n    case s: String => stringTokenizer.tokenize(s)\n    case b: Array[Byte] => stringTokenizer.tokenizeBytes(b)\n\n    case i: Int => numericTokenizer.tokenize(Int.box(i))\n    case l: Long => numericTokenizer.tokenize(Long.box(l))\n    case f: Float => numericTokenizer.tokenize(Float.box(f))\n    case d: Double => numericTokenizer.tokenize(Double.box(d))\n    case bd: java.math.BigDecimal => numericTokenizer.tokenize(bd)\n\n    case ldt: LocalDateTime => temporalTokenizer.tokenize(ldt)\n    case ld: LocalDate => temporalTokenizer.tokenize(ld)\n\n    case other =>\n      // Fallback: convert to string\n      Token(other.toString, TokenType.STRING, Map(\"fallback\" -> true))\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Tokenize a batch of values.\n    *\n    * @param values sequence of values to tokenize\n    * @return iterator of tokens\n    */\n  def tokenizeBatch(values: Iterable[Any]): Iterator[Token] =\n    values.iterator.map(tokenize)\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// JSON Tokenization Support\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n  * Sealed hierarchy for JSON values enabling exhaustive pattern matching.\n  */\nsealed trait JsonValue\n\ncase class JsonString(value: String) extends JsonValue\ncase class JsonNumber(value: Double) extends JsonValue\ncase class JsonBoolean(value: Boolean) extends JsonValue\ncase class JsonArray(values: Vector[JsonValue]) extends JsonValue\ncase class JsonObject(fields: Map[String, JsonValue]) extends JsonValue\ncase object JsonNull extends JsonValue\n\n/**\n  * Tokenizer for JSON structures with recursive type support.\n  *\n  * @param pretty format JSON with indentation\n  */\nclass JsonTokenizer(pretty: Boolean = false) {\n\n  /**\n    * Convert JsonValue to string representation.\n    *\n    * @param json the JsonValue to serialize\n    * @return JSON string\n    */\n  private def jsonToString(json: JsonValue): String = json match {\n    case JsonNull => \"null\"\n    case JsonBoolean(b) => b.toString\n    case JsonString(s) => s\"\"\"\"${escape(s)}\"\"\"\"\n    case JsonNumber(n) => n.toString\n    case JsonArray(vals) =>\n      vals.map(jsonToString).mkString(\"[\", \", \", \"]\")\n    case JsonObject(fields) =>\n      fields.map { case (k, v) =>\n        s\"\"\"\"${escape(k)}\": ${jsonToString(v)}\"\"\"\n      }.mkString(\"{\", \", \", \"}\")\n  }\n\n  private def escape(s: String): String =\n    s.replace(\"\\\\\", \"\\\\\\\\\").replace(\"\\\"\", \"\\\\\\\"\")\n\n  /**\n    * Tokenize a JSON value.\n    *\n    * @param json the JsonValue to tokenize\n    * @return Token with STRUCTURED type\n    */\n  def tokenize(json: JsonValue): Token = {\n    val str = jsonToString(json)\n    Token(\n      str,\n      TokenType.STRUCTURED,\n      Map(\"format\" -> \"json\", \"pretty\" -> pretty)\n    )\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Extract and tokenize a value at a JSON path (dot-separated).\n    *\n    * @param json the JsonValue to traverse\n    * @param path dot-separated path (e.g., \"user.name\")\n    * @return Option containing Token if path is valid\n    */\n  def tokenizePath(json: JsonValue, path: String): Option[Token] = {\n    val parts = path.split(\"\\\\.\")\n    var current: JsonValue = json\n\n    for (part <- parts) {\n      current = current match {\n        case JsonObject(fields) =>\n          fields.getOrElse(part, return None)\n        case JsonArray(vals) =>\n          Try(part.toInt).toOption\n            .flatMap(idx => if (idx >= 0 && idx < vals.length) Some(vals(idx)) else None)\n            .getOrElse(return None)\n        case _ => return None\n      }\n    }\n\n    Some(tokenize(current))\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Whitespace Tokenizer - Basic Text Tokenization\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n  * Basic tokenizer that splits text by whitespace.\n  *\n  * This is a fundamental tokenization operation used in NLP and text processing.\n  *\n  * @param lowercase convert all tokens to lowercase\n  * @param minLength minimum token length (shorter tokens filtered out)\n  * @param maxLength maximum token length (longer tokens truncated)\n  * @param stripPunctuation remove leading/trailing punctuation\n  */\nclass WhitespaceTokenizer(\n  lowercase: Boolean = false,\n  minLength: Int = 0,\n  maxLength: Option[Int] = None,\n  stripPunctuation: Boolean = false\n) {\n  private val punctuation = Set(\".\", \",\", \"!\", \"?\", \";\", \":\", \"'\", \"\\\"\", \"(\", \")\", \"[\", \"]\", \"{\", \"}\")\n\n  /**\n    * Process a single word into a token string or None if filtered.\n    *\n    * @param word the word to process\n    * @return Option containing processed word or None if filtered\n    */\n  private def processToken(word: String): Option[String] = {\n    var processed = word\n\n    if (stripPunctuation) {\n      processed = processed\n        .dropWhile(c => punctuation.contains(c.toString))\n        .reverse\n        .dropWhile(c => punctuation.contains(c.toString))\n        .reverse\n    }\n\n    if (lowercase) {\n      processed = processed.toLowerCase\n    }\n\n    if (processed.length < minLength) {\n      None\n    } else {\n      maxLength match {\n        case Some(max) => Some(processed.take(max))\n        case None => Some(processed)\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Split text by whitespace and return list of tokens.\n    *\n    * @param text input text to tokenize\n    * @return list of Token objects\n    */\n  def tokenize(text: String): Vector[Token] = {\n    val words = text.split(\"\\\\s+\")\n    words.zipWithIndex.flatMap { case (word, position) =>\n      processToken(word).map { processed =>\n        Token(\n          processed,\n          TokenType.STRING,\n          Map(\"position\" -> position, \"original\" -> word)\n        )\n      }\n    }.toVector\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Split text by whitespace and return list of token strings.\n    *\n    * @param text input text to tokenize\n    * @return list of token strings\n    */\n  def tokenizeToStrings(text: String): Vector[String] =\n    tokenize(text).map(_.value)\n\n  /**\n    * Tokenize text and return tokens with character positions.\n    *\n    * @param text input text to tokenize\n    * @return list of tuples (token_string, start_pos, end_pos)\n    */\n  def tokenizeWithPositions(text: String): Vector[(String, Int, Int)] = {\n    text.split(\"\\\\s+\")\n      .foldLeft((Vector.empty[(String, Int, Int)], 0)) { case ((acc, currentPos), word) =>\n        val start = text.indexOf(word, currentPos)\n        val end = start + word.length\n\n        val result = processToken(word) match {\n          case Some(processed) => acc :+ (processed, start, end)\n          case None => acc\n        }\n\n        (result, end)\n      }\n      ._1\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Return the number of tokens in the text.\n    *\n    * @param }.getOrElse(Vector.empty)\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n  * Functor for tokens supporting map and flatMap operations.\n  *\n  * @param value the wrapped value\n  * @tparam T the type of the wrapped value\n values of type T.\n  * Allows accepting supertypes (contravariance).\n  *\n  * @tparam T the type of values (contravariant)\n  */\nclass TokenSink[-T] {\n  private val received = scala.collection.mutable.ListBuffer.empty[Any]\n\n  /**\n    * Receive a value into the sink.\n    *\n    * @param item item to receive\n    */\n  def receive(item: T): Unit = {\n    received += item\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Drain all received items and clear the sink.\n    *\n    * @return immutable vector of all received items\n    */\n  def drain(): Vector[Any] = {\n    val result = received.toVector\n    received.clear()\n    result\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n  * Invariant handler that both produces and consumes values of type T.\n  * Exact type matching required.\n  *\n  * @param default initial value\n  * @tparam T the type of values (invariant)\n  */\nclass BivariantHandler[T](default: T) {\n  private var value: T = default\n\n  /**\n    * Get the current value.\n    *\n    * @return current value\n    */\n  def get: T = value\n\n  /**\n    * Set a new value.\n    *\n    * @param newValue the value to set\n    */\n  def set(newValue: T): Unit = {\n    value = newValue\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Transform the current value using a function.\n    *\n    * @param func transformation function\n    * @return the new value after transformation\n    */\n  def transform(func: T => T): T = {\n    value = func(value)\n    value\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Type Classes for Dynamic Dispatch\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n  * Type class for converting values to tokens.\n  * Enables ad-hoc polymorphism for arbitrary types.\n  *\n  * @tparam A the type that can be tokenized\n  */\ntrait Tokenizable[A] {\n  /**\n    * Convert a value to a Token.\n    *\n    * @param value the value to tokenize\n    * @return the resulting Token\n    */\n  def toToken(value: A): Token\n}\n\nobject Tokenizable {\n  /**\n    * Summon an implicit Tokenizable instance for type A.\n    *\n    * @tparam A the type to tokenize\n    * @return the Tokenizable instance\n    */\n  def apply[A](implicit ev: Tokenizable[A]): Tokenizable[A] = ev\n\n  // Implicit instances for common types\n  implicit val stringTokenizable: Tokenizable[String] =\n    value => Token(value, TokenType.STRING)\n\n  implicit val intTokenizable: Tokenizable[Int] =\n    value => Token(value.toString, TokenType.NUMERIC, Map(\"type\" -> \"int\"))\n\n  implicit val longTokenizable: Tokenizable[Long] =\n    value => Token(value.toString, TokenType.NUMERIC, Map(\"type\" -> \"long\"))\n\n  implicit val doubleTokenizable: Tokenizable[Double] =\n    value => Token(f\"$value%.6f\", TokenType.NUMERIC, Map(\"type\" -> \"double\"))\n\n  implicit val bigDecimalTokenizable: Tokenizable[BigDecimal] =\n    value => Token(value.toString, TokenType.NUMERIC, Map(\"type\" -> \"bigdecimal\"))\n\n  implicit val localDateTimeTokenizable: Tokenizable[LocalDateTime] =\n    value => Token(value.toString, TokenType.TEMPORAL)\n\n  implicit val localDateTokenizable: Tokenizable[LocalDate] =\n    value => Token(value.toString, TokenType.TEMPORAL)\n\n  implicit def optionTokenizable[A: Tokenizable]: Tokenizable[Option[A]] = {\n    case Some(a) => implicitly[Tokenizable[A]].toToken(a)\n    case None => Token(\"NULL\", TokenType.NULL)\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Base Tokenizer Trait\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n  * Abstract base tokenizer with generic input type.\n  *\n  * @tparam T the input type to tokenize\n  */\ntrait BaseTokenizer[T] {\n  /**\n    * Convert a single value to a token.\n    *\n    * @param value the value to tokenize\n    * @return the resulting Token\n    */\n  def tokenize(value: T): Token\n\n  /**\n    * Lazy tokenization of multiple values.\n    *\n    * @param values sequence of values to tokenize\n    * @return iterator of tokens\n    */\n  def tokenizeBatch(values: Iterable[T]): Iterator[Token] =\n    values.iterator.map(tokenize)\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Tokenizer Implementations\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n  * Tokenizer for string and bytes types.\n  *\n  * @param encoding character encoding for bytes (default: UTF-8)\n  * @param normalizer optional function to normalize strings\n  */\nclass StringTokenizer(\n  encoding: String = \"UTF-8\",\n  normalizer: Option[String => String] = None\n) extends BaseTokenizer[String] {\n\n  private val normalize = normalizer.getOrElse((s: String) => s)\n\n  /**\n    * Tokenize a string value.\n    *\n    * @param value the string to tokenize\n    * @return Token with STRING type\n    */\n  override def tokenize(value: String): Token = {\n    val normalized = normalize(value)\n    Token(normalized, TokenType.STRING)\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Tokenize bytes after decoding.\n    *\n    * @param bytes the bytes to tokenize\n    * @return Token with STRING type\n    */\n  def tokenizeBytes(bytes: Array[Byte]): Token = {\n    val str = new String(bytes, encoding)\n    tokenize(str)\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n  * Tokenizer for numeric types with precision handling.\n  *\n  * @param precision decimal places for floating point numbers\n  * @param formatOptions additional formatting options\n  */\nclass NumericTokenizer(\n  precision: Int = 6,\n  formatOptions: Map[String, Any] = Map.empty\n) extends BaseTokenizer[Number] {\n\n  /**\n    * Tokenize a numeric value.\n    *\n    * @param value the number to tokenize\n    * @return Token with NUMERIC type and metadata\n    */\n  override def tokenize(value: Number): Token = {\n    val strValue = value match {\n      case i: java.lang.Integer => i.toString\n      case l: java.lang.Long => l.toString\n      case f: java.lang.Float => f\"${f.doubleValue()}%.${precision}f\"\n      case d: java.lang.Double => f\"$d%.${precision}f\"\n      case bd: java.math.BigDecimal => f\"${bd.doubleValue()}%.${precision}f\"\n      case _ => value.toString\n    }\n\n    Token(\n      strValue,\n      TokenType.NUMERIC,\n      Map(\"original_type\" -> value.getClass.getSimpleName) ++ formatOptions\n    )\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n  * Tokenizer for date/time types.\n  *\n  * @param formatStr optional custom format string\n  */\nclass TemporalTokenizer(formatStr: Option[String] = None) extends BaseTokenizer[java.time.temporal.Temporal] {\n\n  /**\n    * Tokenize a date or time value.\n    *\n    * @param value the date/time to tokenize\n    * @return Token with TEMPORAL type\n    */\n  override def tokenize(value: java.time.temporal.Temporal): Token = {\n    val str = value match {\n      case ldt: LocalDateTime => formatStr.getOrElse(\"yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ss\")\n      case ld: LocalDate => formatStr.getOrElse(\"yyyy-MM-dd\")\n      case _ => formatStr.getOrElse(\"yyyy-MM-dd\")\n    }\n\n    val formatter = java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern(str)\n    Token(formatter.format(value), TokenType.TEMPORAL)\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Universal Tokenizer with Pattern Matching\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n  * Universal tokenizer that handles multiple types through pattern matching.\n  * Uses type classes internally for extensibility.\n  */\nclass UniversalTokenizer {\n  private val stringTokenizer = new StringTokenizer()\n  private val numericTokenizer = new NumericTokenizer()\n  private val temporalTokenizer = new TemporalTokenizer()\n\n  /**\n    * Tokenize any value by dispatching to appropriate tokenizer.\n    *\n    * @param value the value to tokenize\n    * @return Token with appropriate type\n    */\n  def tokenize(value: Any): Token = value match {\n    case null => Token(\"NULL\", TokenType.NULL)\n    case None => Token(\"NULL\", TokenType.NULL)\n    case Some(inner) => tokenize(inner)\n\n    case s: String => stringTokenizer.tokenize(s)\n    case b: Array[Byte] => stringTokenizer.tokenizeBytes(b)\n\n    case i: Int => numericTokenizer.tokenize(Int.box(i))\n    case l: Long => numericTokenizer.tokenize(Long.box(l))\n    case f: Float => numericTokenizer.tokenize(Float.box(f))\n    case d: Double => numericTokenizer.tokenize(Double.box(d))\n    case bd: java.math.BigDecimal => numericTokenizer.tokenize(bd)\n\n    case ldt: LocalDateTime => temporalTokenizer.tokenize(ldt)\n    case ld: LocalDate => temporalTokenizer.tokenize(ld)\n\n    case other =>\n      // Fallback: convert to string\n      Token(other.toString, TokenType.STRING, Map(\"fallback\" -> true))\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Tokenize a batch of values.\n    *\n    * @param values sequence of values to tokenize\n    * @return iterator of tokens\n    */\n  def tokenizeBatch(values: Iterable[Any]): Iterator[Token] =\n    values.iterator.map(tokenize)\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// JSON Tokenization Support\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n  * Sealed hierarchy for JSON values enabling exhaustive pattern matching.\n  */\nsealed trait JsonValue\n\ncase class JsonString(value: String) extends JsonValue\ncase class JsonNumber(value: Double) extends JsonValue\ncase class JsonBoolean(value: Boolean) extends JsonValue\ncase class JsonArray(values: Vector[JsonValue]) extends JsonValue\ncase class JsonObject(fields: Map[String, JsonValue]) extends JsonValue\ncase object JsonNull extends JsonValue\n\n/**\n  * Tokenizer for JSON structures with recursive type support.\n  *\n  * @param pretty format JSON with indentation\n  */\nclass JsonTokenizer(pretty: Boolean = false) {\n\n  /**\n    * Convert JsonValue to string representation.\n    *\n    * @param json the JsonValue to serialize\n    * @return JSON string\n    */\n  private def jsonToString(json: JsonValue): String = json match {\n    case JsonNull => \"null\"\n    case JsonBoolean(b) => b.toString\n    case JsonString(s) => s\"\"\"\"${escape(s)}\"\"\"\"\n    case JsonNumber(n) => n.toString\n    case JsonArray(vals) =>\n      vals.map(jsonToString).mkString(\"[\", \", \", \"]\")\n    case JsonObject(fields) =>\n      fields.map { case (k, v) =>\n        s\"\"\"\"${escape(k)}\": ${jsonToString(v)}\"\"\"\n      }.mkString(\"{\", \", \", \"}\")\n  }\n\n  private def escape(s: String): String =\n    s.replace(\"\\\\\", \"\\\\\\\\\").replace(\"\\\"\", \"\\\\\\\"\")\n\n  /**\n    * Tokenize a JSON value.\n    *\n    * @param json the JsonValue to tokenize\n    * @return Token with STRUCTURED type\n    */\n  def tokenize(json: JsonValue): Token = {\n    val str = jsonToString(json)\n    Token(\n      str,\n      TokenType.STRUCTURED,\n      Map(\"format\" -> \"json\", \"pretty\" -> pretty)\n    )\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Extract and tokenize a value at a JSON path (dot-separated).\n    *\n    * @param json the JsonValue to traverse\n    * @param path dot-separated path (e.g., \"user.name\")\n    * @return Option containing Token if path is valid\n    */\n  def tokenizePath(json: JsonValue, path: String): Option[Token] = {\n    val parts = path.split(\"\\\\.\")\n    var current: JsonValue = json\n\n    for (part <- parts) {\n      current = current match {\n        case JsonObject(fields) =>\n          fields.getOrElse(part, return None)\n        case JsonArray(vals) =>\n          Try(part.toInt).toOption\n            .flatMap(idx => if (idx >= 0 && idx < vals.length) Some(vals(idx)) else None)\n            .getOrElse(return None)\n        case _ => return None\n      }\n    }\n\n    Some(tokenize(current))\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Whitespace Tokenizer - Basic Text Tokenization\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n  * Basic tokenizer that splits text by whitespace.\n  *\n  * This is a fundamental tokenization operation used in NLP and text processing.\n  *\n  * @param lowercase convert all tokens to lowercase\n  * @param minLength minimum token length (shorter tokens filtered out)\n  * @param maxLength maximum token length (longer tokens truncated)\n  * @param stripPunctuation remove leading/trailing punctuation\n  */\nclass WhitespaceTokenizer(\n  lowercase: Boolean = false,\n  minLength: Int = 0,\n  maxLength: Option[Int] = None,\n  stripPunctuation: Boolean = false\n) {\n  private val punctuation = Set(\".\", \",\", \"!\", \"?\", \";\", \":\", \"'\", \"\\\"\", \"(\", \")\", \"[\", \"]\", \"{\", \"}\")\n\n  /**\n    * Process a single word into a token string or None if filtered.\n    *\n    * @param word the word to process\n    * @return Option containing processed word or None if filtered\n    */\n  private def processToken(word: String): Option[String] = {\n    var processed = word\n\n    if (stripPunctuation) {\n      processed = processed\n        .dropWhile(c => punctuation.contains(c.toString))\n        .reverse\n        .dropWhile(c => punctuation.contains(c.toString))\n        .reverse\n    }\n\n    if (lowercase) {\n      processed = processed.toLowerCase\n    }\n\n    if (processed.length < minLength) {\n      None\n    } else {\n      maxLength match {\n        case Some(max) => Some(processed.take(max))\n        case None => Some(processed)\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Split text by whitespace and return list of tokens.\n    *\n    * @param text input text to tokenize\n    * @return list of Token objects\n    */\n  def tokenize(text: String): Vector[Token] = {\n    val words = text.split(\"\\\\s+\")\n    words.zipWithIndex.flatMap { case (word, position) =>\n      processToken(word).map { processed =>\n        Token(\n          processed,\n          TokenType.STRING,\n          Map(\"position\" -> position, \"original\" -> word)\n        )\n      }\n    }.toVector\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Split text by whitespace and return list of token strings.\n    *\n    * @param text input text to tokenize\n    * @return list of token strings\n    */\n  def tokenizeToStrings(text: String): Vector[String] =\n    tokenize(text).map(_.value)\n\n  /**\n    * Tokenize text and return tokens with character positions.\n    *\n    * @param text input text to tokenize\n    * @return list of tuples (token_string, start_pos, end_pos)\n    */\n  def tokenizeWithPositions(text: String): Vector[(String, Int, Int)] = {\n    text.split(\"\\\\s+\")\n      .foldLeft((Vector.empty[(String, Int, Int)], 0)) { case ((acc, currentPos), word) =>\n        val start = text.indexOf(word, currentPos)\n        val end = start + word.length\n\n        val result = processToken(word) match {\n          case Some(processed) => acc :+ (processed, start, end)\n          case None => acc\n        }\n\n        (result, end)\n      }\n      ._1\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Return the number of tokens in the text.\n    *\n    * @param text input text\n    * @return token count\n    */\n  def countTokens(text: String): Int =\n    tokenize(text).length\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n  * Registry for managing multiple tokenizers and their handlers.\n  * Demonstrates complex nested generic types.\n  *\n  * @tparam T the type of items being tokenized\n  */\nclass TokenRegistry[T] {\n  private val registry = scala.collection.mutable.Map.empty[String, TokenContainer[T]]\n  private val handlers = }.getOrElse(Vector.empty)\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n  * Functor for tokens supporting map and flatMap operations.\n  *\n  * @param value the wrapped value\n  * @tparam T the type of the wrapped value\n `Generic[T_co]` (covariant), `Generic[T_contra]` (contravariant)\n- **Scala**: `[+T]` for covariance, `[-T]` for contravariance\n- **Advantage**: Scala's variance annotations are more concise and def tokenize(value: T): Token\n\n  def tokenizeBatch(values: Iterable[T]): Iterator[Token] =\n    values.iterator.map(tokenize)\n}\n```\n\n### 6. **Specific Tokenizers**\n- **StringTokenizer**: runtime_checkable\n- **Scala**: Type class trait with implicit instances\n- **Advantage**: Compile-time checked, better for distributed systems\n\n```scala\ntrait Tokenizable[A] {\n  def Right(Token(normalized, TokenType.STRING, metadata))\n  }\n}\n```\n\n### 9. **JSON Tokenization**\n- **Python**: Recursive type alias `JsonValue`\n- **Scala**: Sealed algebraic data type with
